- **command-args-validation.doctest**: Every command validates its untyped `args` bag through `parseCommandArgs` (`command-runner.ts`) — the dispatch boundary. A wrong-typed field is a `CommandArgsError`, surfaced by `runCommand` as a clean `{ success: false }` result naming the field — never silently dropped, never a stack trace.
